- **1914.01** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-shadow-bows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  煤气灯芯拧到最小，天花板上一件挂着的大衣被风推得晃了晃。Klara躺在摇篮里忽然笑出声，朝那片影子伸出两只手。Josef Bauer抬头看了很久，只看见衣钩和一块掉漆的墙皮。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +laughed_at_ceiling_shadow_

- **1915.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Katharina Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Katharina Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1916.05** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1916.08** `statistical_recovery`

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Klara的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Klara在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000006; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1918.07** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Klara扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Klara摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Klara一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Maria Bauer一挣，Klara只能把Maria Bauer抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Klara背着Maria Bauer在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Maria Bauer擦鼻涕。等Maria Bauer睡沉，Klara的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Gustav Weiss把Klara拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Klara把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Klara蹲下把两包并排，按Gustav Weiss说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Klara正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Maria Bauer挡穿堂风。Maria Bauer在旧布底下翻了个身，Klara立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Katharina Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Maria Bauer，再看见Klara冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001011:child; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1923.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Klara弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Klara把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Katharina Bauer把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Klara每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/no-vacancy` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Klara问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1924.05** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Klara把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.03_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.010000000000000009; skill_

- **1927.12** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Klara推刨子。Klara推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Klara，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Katharina Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  Klara把煤油灯芯捻到最亮，复习算术到后半夜，德文作文列了三段提纲。第二天考场上算错一道比例题，但短文写得清楚，Luise Huber收卷时多看了他两眼。

  Klara没有寄信，把信塞进Hermann Kral 2常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Otto Lechner说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Klara去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1928.08** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Huber把一本拉丁文课本推到Klara面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Klara翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Klara把纸抄了一遍，塞进Anton的历史作业本；那天晚上，Klara第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood 0.03_

  Klara把手套交给Anton，让Anton替自己去还。Anton回来时说，Elisabeth Bauer问是谁捡到的；Anton顺口报了一个名字，Klara听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。

  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +dropout_confirmed_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。

- **1934.01** `state_tick:political-suspect/police-interest/avoided` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick
  Klara换了路线，避开那家咖啡馆和门口的两个便衣。档案里又添了一页，但今年没有传唤。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.7000000000000001 -> 0.65_

- **1934.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreich出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005430_

- **1934.06** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/mistaken-identity` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, crisis
  一个代表团走错了房间，把Klara当成新来的部长，围着说了十分钟的请求。Klara没有纠正。等人走后，Klara坐在那张不属于自己的椅子上，听见走廊里有人在找真正的部长。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; reputation 0.03999999999999998; state_add stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:287; flag +mistaken_for_official; flag -stage_sovereign; flag +stage_king; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1934.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Luise Weiss。接生的人收六克朗，Josef Weiss在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:327_

- **1935.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/took-over-a-club` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  一个快要散掉的政治俱乐部让Klara接手。会员只剩十一个人，欠着两个月房租，但那间办公室的门牌还挂着。Klara在第一次会上没有讲理想，只把账本翻开。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; organization_found 接手后的俱乐部 (party); position 临时主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:005699_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005699_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:political-suspect/police-interest/detained` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick
  Klara在回家路上被拦住，问话从晚上八点持续到凌晨。没有正式指控，但名字被写进了另一本册子。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.65 -> 0.8; mood -0.07999999999999996_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `state_expire:stage-king` tags: stage-king, performance, theatre, status, irony, state, state_tick, state:stage-king
  Klara的「舞台上的国王」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:287; flag -stage_king_

- **1935.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/housing-relative.lodged-elsewhere` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  Klara在楼里替教父Alois Berg问了一圈，最后在洗衣妇家找到一张空床，每月三克朗。Alois Berg把箱子搬过去，Klara替他垫了第一个月的房租；两人在楼梯口分开，Alois Berg说等找到工就还。床板短了一截，箱子只能竖着放。
  _effects: cash -3 (first month’s bed rent for the relative); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000264:child; role; role; flag +relative_lodged_elsewhere; knowledge “洗衣妇家有一张空床，每月三克朗。”_

- **1935.08** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cut-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, vienna
  放映到第十一本，胶片在片门里卷成一团。Klara剪掉烧焦的一截，发现后面接的是一卷没送审的新闻片：军靴踏过广场，镜头扫过一张张被按下去的脸。Gustav Weiss 2在黑暗里说“接回正片”，Klara照做了。
  _effects: organization_found 小巷电影院 (household_firm); position 放映员 @cinema:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005702; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:328; cash 1 (projectionist wages); skill; flag +cut_the_reel; knowledge “第十一本胶片里夹着没送审的新闻片，{projectionist}让我接回正片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1935.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Klara把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Katharina Weiss。接生的人收六克朗，Josef Weiss在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:357_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Klara听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 26; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006210_

- **1936.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Gustav Kral 2在门口堵了三天，第四天把Klara的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Klara把办公室的钥匙交给Josefine Hofer，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 委员会主席 @org-sdap; reputation -0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

- **1936.01** `state_transition:political-suspect/arrested` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick, detained
  Klara从「政治嫌疑」转入另一段处境：the file became an arrest
  _effects: state_transition political-suspect -> detained; flag +political_arrest; flag +detained_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Luise Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Luise Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 45; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1936.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Klara原定在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.10** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fever-hand` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  一连三天的高烧退下去以后，Klara的手腕上多了一块暗褐色的印子，边缘像五根很短的手指。诊所的医生说只是皮下色素，Alois Moser却在院子里看了很久，说这形状不是随便长的。Klara把袖子拉下来，回家路上一直用另一只手按着那块皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (去诊所问这块印子); health -0.030000000000000027 (高烧后的虚弱); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:358; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_fever; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004697_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/child-death-in-the-household` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Klara没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 25; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 65; flag +lost_child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006673_

  Klara在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Alois Bauer 2第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Josefine Hofer发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @cinema:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005702; organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Rosa Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

  一年过去，Klara已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Klara也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 19 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 26 -> 31; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Klara把Katharina Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  又过了一年，Klara学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Klara的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 25 -> 19; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 5 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1937.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1937.05** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/small-part` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  Hedwig只给了Klara三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Klara把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Klara，但乐队指挥记住了Klara每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:399; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:400_

- **1937.08** `r2-law.lawyer-fees::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/retainer-from-pawnshop` tags: law, lawyer, fees, debt, court, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Kral把委托书推过来，说先交二十克朗，开庭另算。Klara去当铺押了Josef Weiss的缝纫机，回来时钱还差三克朗；Katharina Kral把数目写在纸角，说可以欠着，但每拖一周加一克朗。Josef Weiss知道后，把当票收进了自己的口袋。
  _effects: cash -18 (按小时计费的沉默); condition; flag +paid_retainer_with_pawned_sewing_machine; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “the lawyer charges one kronen a week on unpaid fees”_

  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 31 -> 39; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 24 -> 21; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging); condition_

- **1938.01** `major-family.child-death/child-death.measles` tags: family, child_death, grief, major, lifecycle, death, injury, partnership
  麻疹先从耳后起，后来全身都是红点。Klara把窗帘拉上，怕光刺着孩子的眼睛；Josef Weiss从药房带回一包退热粉，Katharina Weiss喝下去就吐了。药房的人说退热粉不能多用，Josef Weiss还是买了第二包。下葬那天下着雨，Klara把一只小鞋留在坟边，鞋底还沾着屋里的煤灰。
  _effects: death (measles and pneumonia); health -0.04999999999999999 (nursing a child through measles); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; role; cash -6 (medicine, coffin and burial)_

  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Helene Lechner排在前面，回头替Klara看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Klara用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1938.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Emil Hofer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Emil Hofer敲开Klara的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Helene Weiss。接生的人收六克朗，Josef Weiss在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:472_

  肉摊的Josef看见Klara挽袖子，刀停在半空，说这种印子像吊死的人留下的手印。旁边买菜的人安静了一瞬，又各自低头挑菜。Klara想笑，却发现自己的手在抖；那天回家，Klara绕开了平时走的巷子。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 13 -> 28; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed illness -> curse; reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +df18_mark_read_as_curse; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Anna Lechner起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anton Bauer 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1941.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Josef Weiss先把Klara抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Klara从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Klara自己。散会后，有人让Klara回家休息几天，工资照发。Klara知道这不是照顾，是让Klara别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

  画店老板把Klara的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Klara抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 69 -> 76; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 10 -> 9; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 59 -> 56; mood -0.030000000000000027; health -0.020000000000000018 (冬天阁楼里没有足够的煤)_

- **1942.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Klara找到Friedrich Weiss，把接手后的俱乐部的章程和会费说清楚；对方留下地址，答应下次再来。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “我通过Klara加入了接手后的俱乐部。”_

- **1942.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Klara把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Klara就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1942.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Katharina Bauer问起工钱和房租，Klara报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Mitzi Moser低着头把面包掰成小块，Katharina Bauer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000006; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000437:child_

- **1942.11** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::glass-and-blood/second-trail-to-the-cellar` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara在院墙下面发现另一串鞋印，比死者的鞋小两号，一直通到Maria Winter家的煤窖。Alois Bauer 2让人把窖门打开，里面只有一排腌菜缸。Maria Winter站在门口说“你们随便看”，手却一直攥着围裙角。
  _effects: condition; flag +found_a_second_trail; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “a second set of footprints leads to the neighbor’s coal cell”_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Klara的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Klara看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007851_

  照片登出后，Klara收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Klara去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:613; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010087_

  一个男孩翻过熊池的矮栏去捡帽子。Klara没有喊，怕惊动母熊，只提着装苹果的桶绕到下风处，把桶敲得咚咚响。母熊转身时，孩子被Josef Bauer 2一把拽了上来；Klara的腿在后来的很多年里还会在梦里发软。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; cash 2 (reward from the child's parents); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:614; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +saved_child_at_zoo; knowledge “母熊被苹果桶引开时，孩子已经挂在{keeper}的胳膊上了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1943.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Rosa Winter攥着Klara的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1943.09** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Klara让Emil冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Anna Moser 2寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Klara把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:615; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  一年过去，Klara已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Klara也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 43; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 23; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Klara学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Klara的材料齐全。年底，Klara从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 35; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Alois Kral 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1944.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Emil Weiss追出来问怎么不领了，Klara说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Klara把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; food_

- **1944.12** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/opened-prison` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara跟着Hermann Kral 2去打开监狱，牢房里的人瘦得站不起来。Berta Berg 2在门口喊一个名字，没人应；Klara在墙上找到一排刻痕，最后一道只刻了一半。Anna Moser 3进来清点，说这些人要先送去医院，不能直接回家。
  _effects: flag +opened_prison_on_liberation_day; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; knowledge “a half-carved mark was left on the cell wall”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1945.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Alois Kral 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Alois Kral 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010631_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Klara躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Klara去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1945.01** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Klara把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Berta Stein 3递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

- **1945.01** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Luise 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Klara把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 43 -> 35; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 60_

  一个税务员把Klara的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Klara收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Klara先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 39 -> 46; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 35 -> 45; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

  Klara刚提一句“那年分东西”，Anna Bauer就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Anna Bauer起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Klara一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; cash -0.4 (付了两份饭钱)_

  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Anna Moser 2要Klara签字认账。Klara知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Anna Moser 2的小舅子。Klara不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:750; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  话没说完就变成了争。Marie Huber把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Klara说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Anna Bauer在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Klara的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。

  Klara没有收回话，也没有开枪。Klara把枪递给Mitzi Moser，说让法院来判。Alois Kral 2笑了，说法院判不了面子。第二天，Klara在报纸上读到自己的名字，标题是“体面人的新体面”。Mitzi Moser把枪盒还回店里，老板说这个月已经退了三副。

- **1955.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/care-sick-parent.hospital` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, vienna
  Klara把Katharina Bauer送进市立医院，Katharina Bauer不肯，抓住床栏说死也要死在家里。Klara还是签了字；病房里六张床，Katharina Bauer被安排在靠门的一张，探视时间一到，护士就把家属往外赶。签字笔是借来的，笔尖分叉，名字写得比平时大。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; cash -14 (hospital deposit and ward fees); role; flag +parent_in_hospital; knowledge “市立医院每天只许探视一小时。”_

  Klara先给Josef盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Josef吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。

- **1914.07** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Rosa Lechner提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Mitzi被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.05_

- **1916.04** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Mitzi烧得翻来覆去，Alois Lechner把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Mitzi偏头躲，Alois Lechner就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.030000000000000027 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1917.04** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Alois Lechner，楼里的Josef Bauer正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Alois Lechner把裹着布条的Mitzi往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Maria Weiss一挣，Mitzi只能把Maria Weiss抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Mitzi背着Maria Weiss在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Maria Weiss擦鼻涕。等Maria Weiss睡沉，Mitzi的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。

- **1928.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-resented-drilling` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Maria Lechner把铅笔一摔，说再也不想看见豆子。Mitzi把豆子一颗颗收进碗里，说随你。那天晚上，Maria Lechner自己把错题改了一遍，改完把本子推到Mitzi面前，不说话。Mitzi看了一遍，在页角画了一个很小的勾。

- **1917.07** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Josef的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Mitzi Lechner不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Josef把弹珠举到眼前看，Mitzi Lechner在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1929.01** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Josef趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Josef说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:107; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.039999999999999994; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Josef又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 5 -> 25; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1929.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-resented-drilling` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Maria Bauer把铅笔一摔，说再也不想看见豆子。Josef把豆子一颗颗收进碗里，说随你。那天晚上，Maria Bauer自己把错题改了一遍，改完把本子推到Josef面前，不说话。Josef看了一遍，在页角画了一个很小的勾。
  _effects: flag +sibling_resented_tutoring; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001011:child_

- **1929.03** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/go-alone` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Bauer把车票放在桌上，说家里给她找了活，走不了。Josef把两张票叠在一起，撕掉一张，另一张塞进内袋；Elisabeth Bauer问Josef一个人怕不怕，Josef说不怕，出门时却把地址写给了她。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 70 -> 80; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 35 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 20; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +yf18_go_alone; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1929.03** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Josef把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Josef没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 80 -> 78_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003120_

- **1929.09** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Josef把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Josef那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Josef上一个东家是谁。Josef说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Josef被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Josef站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Luise Winter没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Josef回头看了一眼，Luise Winter正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1930.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/nose-returns` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Josef感冒了三天，鼻子像塞了棉花。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把十二只小瓶排在柜台上，让Josef闭眼闻；前十一只都答错了，最后一只，Josef说出“佛手柑和一点旧木头”。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把那只瓶子推到一边，说“记住这个味道，它比你的名字值钱”。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:137; skill; reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “第十二只瓶子是佛手柑和旧木头，师傅说这个味道比名字值钱。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Josef把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Josef没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 28 -> 36; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 78 -> 76_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003457_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/route-memorised` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Josef在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 40 -> 52; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 36 -> 38; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Josef废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，他把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Josef又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health -0.09999999999999998 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1930.12** `ds17-courtship.parlour::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-sunday` tags: love, courtship, intimacy, secret, class, secret_intimacy, vienna, reform
  Josef在约瑟夫城租下一间带钢琴的会客室，每小时一克朗，女房东把钥匙拴在一截蓝线上。Gustav Weiss来时带着一束用报纸包好的紫罗兰，Anna Moser坐在窗边打毛线，偶尔抬头看一眼钟。第三个星期日，Anna Moser说自己要去楼下买咖啡，把门留了半掩。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:138; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; cash -1 (the parlour, the coffee and a bunch of violets); mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +rented_a_parlour; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +secret_attachment_active_

- **1931.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/customer-allergy` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  Josef Bauer 2试了新到的紫罗兰香精，脖颈当天起了一圈红疹。Josef认出那批香精兑了工业酒精，追到门口把人叫回来，退了钱，又写了一张配方让药房配温和的。Josef Bauer 2后来带来一篮李子，说是自家树上结的。
  _effects: cash -1 (refunded the harsh violet essence); reputation 0.02999999999999997; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; skill; flag +warned_allergy; knowledge “那批紫罗兰香精兑了工业酒精，闻起来甜，擦在皮肤上会起疹。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:107; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Josef已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Josef没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 5 -> 20; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 0 -> 5; reputation -0.04000000000000001_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:apprenticeship-contract` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick, state:apprenticeship-contract
  Josef的「学徒契约」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:72; role_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Josef侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1931.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Josef当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1931.08** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef对Lena说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Lena把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1931.11** `p10-work-career.first-position/p10-work-career.first-position.hired` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, first_job
  Josef把地址纸条递给门房，门房在招工簿上写下他的名字。试工两天，他跟着Emil Lechner给车床油箱上油，手指沾满黑油。小巷电影院在周末把他留下，说下周起按周发薪，先领八克朗，中午在厂里吃一顿。
  _effects: employment 机器厂上油工 @ cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40-evt:world-vienna-1912:003761; cash 8 (第一周工钱); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Josef的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 32; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 50; mood -0.03; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/still-hidden` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Josef在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 20 -> 15; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 50 -> 53; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef把Maria Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 62 -> 59; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fever-worsens` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, reform, medical
  到了后半夜，Maria Lechner的呼吸越来越急，胸口凹下去一块。Josef把最后两克朗塞给邻居，让邻居去请医生；医生天亮才来，只说怎么不早点送医院。Josef抱着孩子坐在门槛上，等一辆借来的车。
  _effects: cash -5 (第三遍换湿布); health -0.020000000000000018 (第三遍换湿布); flag +child_fever_worsened; health -0.09999999999999998 (the fever worsened before a doctor arrived); condition_

- **1932.03** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, reform
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Josef的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Elisabeth Berg 3给了Josef两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Josef把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); health -0.040000000000000036 (滚筒造成的烫伤); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:202; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1932.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Emil一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Emil的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1932.08** `r2-faith.pew-rent::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stood-at-the-back` tags: faith, church, service, class, shame, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef没有交钱，抱着赞美诗站到洗礼堂门口。Luise Huber把号牌从Josef手里抽回去，说座位是给按时交费的人留的。Anton往长椅里挪了挪，拍身边的位置；Josef摇了摇头，整场礼拜都看着门上的铜把手。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +stood_at_back_of_church; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the sexton keeps a list of pew rent debtors”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1933.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Emil家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Emil那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004267_

- **1933.01** `df18-mark.reading-at-market::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-as-blessing` tags: body, mark, omen, rumor, superstition, market, reform, medical
  市场尽头的Luise Winter会看手相。Josef把铜币放在桌上，Luise Winter捏着手腕看了半天，说这不是病，是“有人替你许过愿”，还送了一条红绳。Josef把红绳系在袖口里，走出棚子时，觉得路上的人都在看自己的手。
  _effects: cash -1 (看手相的铜币); state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 45; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed unknown -> blessing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +df18_mark_read_as_blessing; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  Josef的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:137; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

  Josef把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Josef在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。

- **1933.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith

  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Josef站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; skill_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.05000000000000002_

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  肉摊的Anton Kral看见Josef挽袖子，刀停在半空，说这种印子像吊死的人留下的手印。旁边买菜的人安静了一瞬，又各自低头挑菜。Josef想笑，却发现自己的手在抖；那天回家，Josef绕开了平时走的巷子。

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 30 -> 25; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 15 -> 7; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 8 -> 20; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 0; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Gustav Weiss 2在学徒证上签字，笔尖在“满师”两个字上停了一下。Grete Hofer让Josef把一只燕尾榫拆开再装回去，Josef用了十一分钟。考场外，Rosa Huber问题目难不难，Josef把刨子递过去，说先把刃磨直。
  _effects: cash 2 (师傅签字的学徒证); flag +apprenticeship; role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “a dovetail joint can be taken apart and reset in eleven minu”; education apprenticeship_

  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work_

  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; reputation 0.03_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter_

  _effects: cash -2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_passed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001011:child_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Josef在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Josef低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 100 -> 100; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 44 -> 50; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.inherit-business::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/debts-came-with-it` tags: commerce, inheritance, family_firm, debt, succession, vienna, urban, crisis
  钥匙交到手里第二天，供货商就拿着欠条上门。Katharina Bauer说那些账是铺子的，不是他的；供货商说铺子现在归Josef，账也归Josef。Josef把存货盘了一遍，能抵的抵，剩下十克朗写了还款计划；印章还在，但盖下去的第一份文件是欠条。
  _effects: cash -10 (父亲的账本和印章); condition; flag +work13_inherited_business_with_debts; role; employment Kaufmann @ org-allgemeines-krankenhaus_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007054_

- **1937.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-watched-tools` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  工头第二次出来时眼皮都没抬。Marie Huber把工具往Josef脚边一放，说去去就回，回来时用围裙兜着面包，汤罐用旧布裹着，两人在墙根分着吃完。
  _effects: food_

- **1937.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; health -0.04 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1937.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Gustav Berg没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007176_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1938.01** `state_expire:df18-uncanny-mark` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Josef的「不祥的印记」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:202; flag +df18_mark_faded_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef把Mitzi Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Mitzi Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Lechner 2把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Josef去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Hermann Kral 2给Josef一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Josef把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.06000000000000005 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1938.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1938.08** `hist15-purge.seed::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-name-is-read` tags: purge, repression, organization, surveillance, history, work, vienna, urban
  处里开会时，名单被念到一半。Josef听见自己的名字后面跟着“待审查”，也听见Hermann Kral 2的椅子在地上拖了一下。散会后，走廊尽头多了一张空桌，抽屉里还留着别人的铅笔。
  _effects: state_add institution-purge state:world-vienna-1912:436; mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +saw_the_purge_list_

- **1938.12** `r1-fam.neighbor-solidarity::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::solidarity-war/solidarity.coal-fire` tags: social, neighbor, fire, solidarity, disaster, vienna, urban, crisis
  战时的煤配给不够，有人在走廊里用木柴生火，火星引着了堆在楼梯下的旧报纸。Josef提着水桶冲上去时，Josefine Hofer正把一袋配给面粉往窗外递。火灭后，两家人的眉毛都燎焦了；Josefine Hofer看着那袋面粉，说这袋面够吃到月底。
  _effects: cash -2 (全楼的水桶); health -0.06000000000000005 (全楼的水桶); flag +put_out_a_coal_shortage_fire; role; relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “配给煤烧完了，有人在走廊里烧木柴。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Josef没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007309_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, injury
  Josef在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Emil Bauer 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Josef带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1939.01** `hs18-purge.ration-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/warn-the-family` tags: purge, ration, organization, denunciation, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Josef趁午休跑到Hermann Kral 4家，说今天别去窗口，名单上已经没有了。Hermann Kral 4把桌上的面包收进柜子，问是谁划的。Josef没有回答。第二天，Emil Bauer 2在办公室门口等着，手里拿着那张名单，边上多了一道折痕。
  _effects: state_parameter institution-purge.intensity 0.5800000000000001 -> 0.6200000000000001; state_parameter institution-purge.denunciations 8 -> 11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; knowledge “泄密会留下折痕。”; flag +warned_family_about_ration_list; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.sickness-insurance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/missed-the-deadline` tags: health, insurance, work, stamps, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef等到月底才想起保险册还在抽屉里，印花已经过期。Hermann Huber说下个月可以补，但要重新体检。Josef把册子翻到空白页，看见前面一栏写着“自愿放弃”。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +insurance_enrolment_lapsed; knowledge “a lapsed stamp can be renewed only after a new medical exami”_

- **1939.03** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-newsagent-kept-the-ticket` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  开奖后，Josef Bauer 2从柜台下面把票拿出来，说这张是用赊账买的，按老规矩中奖一人一半。Josef在店里站到天黑，最后拿了三十五克朗；Josef Bauer 2把另一半锁进铁盒，顺手把柜台上的欠账本翻到Josef那一页，划掉了最后一笔。回家路上，Rosa说这比不中强，Josef没有说话。
  _effects: cash 35 (the subject's half of the disputed lottery win); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:471; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; flag +ticket_held_on_credit; knowledge “烟草铺老板把赊账买的彩票扣在柜台下面，直到开奖才拿出来。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.05** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Josef让Rosa Moser冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Alois Bauer 2寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Josef把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:470; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-wwii-conscription/drafted-again` tags: war, conscription, adulthood, unemployment, migration
  Alois Moser把新的征召令拍在桌上，Josef只有一天时间收拾。Josef把工票、钥匙和一张全家照片交给家里，第二天到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang报到。
  _effects: role; unemployment (conscripted for war); move place-wien-landwehr-barracks; cash 6 (family allowance)_

- **1940.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Alois Moser把黑皮本翻到Josef那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Anna Stein 2在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Josef当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 30 -> 22; reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +taxes_paid; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Josef刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Josef站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008386_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Josef的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Josef家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008401_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Josef的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Josef收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Josef先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 62; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Josef把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Josef来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Franz Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.no-baptism` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  “洗礼证明。”Helene Winter 2把手一摊。Josef只有出生纸，说教区在城另一头。站务员看了眼钟，说名册按时候收。他跑出去搭车，回来时柜门已经上锁。
  _effects: cash -1 (往返教区的车钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 18; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008685_

- **1940.06** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Rosa Huber在午休时把本子摊在Josef的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Rosa Huber当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1940.06** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Josef的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Josef家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008389_

- **1940.09** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-dies-at-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子出生时已经没有呼吸。Josef去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗，回来时Rosa还躺在床上看着天花板；邻居把借来的摇篮搬走了，Josef成了丧子的父亲，屋里没人知道该说什么，只有炉子还在响。
  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

- **1941.01** `major-family.separation-widowhood/separation-widowhood.widowed-tin-box` tags: family, separation, widowhood, housing, major, lifecycle, death, injury
  Rosa咳了半年，最后一个月连楼梯都上不去。Josef把床让给对方，自己睡在厨房的长凳上。Rosa死在清晨，Josef去市政办事处登记，又去Rosa上工的地方结清工钱；葬礼从简，神父念完经，Josef把Rosa的工票和剃刀收进一只铁盒。
  _effects: death (pulmonary tuberculosis); health -0.06000000000000005 (nursing and grief before the funeral); role; cash 8 (final wages collected from the partner's workplace); cash -12 (funeral, mass and black clothes)_

- **1941.01** `bc18-ghost.quiet-correction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-army/school-inspector` tags: bureaucracy, paperwork, identity, absurd, legal, quiet, comic, vienna
  学务督察按着新户籍找上门，说有一名适龄儿童从未入学。Josef躲在阁楼里，Hermann Kral在楼下说孩子出疹子，不能见风；督察留下了一张入学通知和一瓶鱼肝油。晚上，Josef从阁楼下来，看见通知上写着“七岁”，忽然觉得自己这一生确实该重新开始。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 62 -> 77; reputation -0.01999999999999999; condition; flag +school_notice_for_newborn_

- **1941.01** `state_transition:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/ghost-becomes-a-case` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Josef从「档案里的活鬼」转入另一段处境：档案风险积累到足以让身份混乱变成刑事案件
  _effects: state_transition bc18-bureaucratic-ghost -> under-investigation; reputation -0.05000000000000002; flag +ghost_investigated; flag -bureaucratic_ghost_

  老太太接过孩子，用旧围裙兜着坐在窗边，说一克朗一周，六点送来六点接。Josef数出硬币，把奶瓶放在她灶台边。第三夜孩子还是哭醒，Josef抱着他在屋里走，天亮前靠着床沿打了个盹。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; health -0.030000000000000027 (孩子夜里哭醒，{subject}睡眠不足)_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008956_

- **1941.08** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.returned-sugar-box/sugar-box-go-in-person` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Josef亲自去Anton Bauer 2家，把话说清：糖盒既退回来，以后不再托人登门。Anton Bauer 2的母亲站在门里，说这都是为自家孩子打算，语气不重也不轻。Josef把帽子戴正，没有纠缠，下楼时一步一步数着台阶；门房和邻居都看在眼里，这一回至少把话说在了明处。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1941.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Josef Bauer 4先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Josef点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1942.01** `cj17-police.lineup::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-army/identified` tags: police, court, legal, investigation, mistaken_identity, humiliation, vienna, urban
  Grete Hofer在第一个人面前就站住了，说那件灰外套不会错。Hermann Huber没有再看Josef的眼睛，只让警士把手铐拿出来。Josef被推上囚车时，听见Grete Hofer在身后说：“我记得那个人走路的样子。”其实Josef的左脚并不跛。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (被警士推上囚车时撞到车门); state_add detained state:world-vienna-1912:572; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; flag +identified_by_witness; mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +detained_

  Josef坐在后厅角落的啤酒箱上听完整场排练，Anton中途走过来问要不要在名册上签字，Josef说再想想。散场时人们三三两两去前厅续杯，Josef把空杯子放回柜台，顺着酒馆后门的巷子走回家，嘴里还留着半段旋律；这一晚没花钱，也和Anton多说了两句话。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1942.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/ledger-on-table` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Elisabeth Bauer的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Josef在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: flag +medicine_bill_settled_

- **1942.08** `hs18-radio.rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-army/switch-it-off` tags: radio, blackout, rumor, panic, neighbor, politics, vienna, urban
  Josef伸手关掉收音机，说电池要留给夜里的急救。Emil Weiss不同意，两人在黑暗里摸到同一只旋钮。最后谁也没有再开；楼道里只剩邻居的怀表在走，每一下都像在催人下楼。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:573; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +silenced_radio_during_blackout_

- **1942.08** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Josef把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Josef Bauer 4把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007310_

- **1943.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Helene Hofer的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Helene Hofer算了一夜，第二天把Josef送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Emil Hofer在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Emil Hofer在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1943.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-army/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef带着Maria Bauer去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Maria Bauer一直捏着那张课程表，Josef则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 21 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  半夜的空袭把屋顶掀开一角。Josef和邻居把家具拖到楼道，天亮后去市政办事处登记损失。窗户用木板钉上，一家人挤到地下室。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; health -0.05 (air raid and cold); condition; cash -6 (repairs and moving（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1944.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Josef就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Grete Hofer摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1944.10** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/allocate-by-need` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Grete Hofer说三号院的老太太咳得直不起腰，Josef把一个木箱底的碎煤单独拢出来，用报纸包好。借条上写的是两千克朗，其实只值几赫勒的煤，但手印按得很重。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1944.11** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/quarrel-with-clerk` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Josef和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Josef在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  Josef开口借钱读夜校，Maria Bauer坐在新买的沙发上，说家里也有难处，孩子要上学。Josef看见茶几上放着一盒没拆的进口糖，没有提。临走时，Maria Bauer送到门口，说以后少来往，省得两边都为难。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +sibling_refused_help; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001011:child_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: cash 3 (帮鞋匠搬家的工钱)_

  _effects: cash -3 (按月先付一部分房租与旧欠煤账（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood -0.02000000000000013; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

  Josef把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Josef睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition_

- **1947.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef请楼下的Emil Weiss白天来照看Katharina Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Emil Weiss上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Josef下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Katharina Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; health 0.040000000000000036 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Josef把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Emil Hofer把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood -0.08999999999999997_

  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000290:child; knowledge “卫生巡查表上每户只能签自己那一栏，代签要负连带责任”_

- **1948.04** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Anna Bauer追出来问怎么不领了，Josef说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Josef把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; food_

- **1948.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  汤勺在锅里搅了两圈，Josef把熏肉块拨到Elisabeth Bauer碗里。热气模糊了窗玻璃，街上的圣诞摊子传来铃铛声；Elisabeth Bauer吃得很慢，碗底最后一点汤也用面包擦干净。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.07** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +wish:craft:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013183_

- **1948.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Josef Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Josef Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Josef和Anton Kral按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Josef喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1950.03** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Alois Kral 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Alois Kral 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; flag +parish_refused_aid; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1951.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Josef自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1951.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/hospital-trip` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Josef看老人喘得厉害，决定送医院。他先到工头那里请一天假，工头没抬头，只说不来就按缺勤记。押金要六克朗，Josef从抽屉里凑出三克朗，又向柜上赊了剩下的，推着借来的手推车把人送到医院门口，一路颠得老人抓着车沿不松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院押金（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.06000000000000005 (送医后得到诊治)_

- **1952.01** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Helene Lechner敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Helene Lechner什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.read-in-aisle` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Josef站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Josef Bauer 4在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Josef摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1952.04** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Gustav Kral 3凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Josef把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food_

- **1952.11** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Emil Lechner没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1953.01** `p10-migration-tasks.return-home/return-stop-in-brno` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, return, train
  车票钱只够坐到布吕恩。他在车站帮人搬箱子、给旅馆跑腿，换了几顿饭和半张到家乡的票，晚上睡在候车室的长椅上。到家时靴底磨穿了一个洞，他拄着一根捡来的木棍走进院子。
  _effects: cash -7 (到布吕恩的车票与半张后续车票（其中7克朗记入债务）); skill; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-163_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.sugar-jar` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef把信压在厨房的糖罐底下，想着明天再问。三天后糖罐渗了油，信角洇出一片深色，收信人的名字糊了半个。她把信拿到门房那儿，门房说这地址已经退过两回了。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1954.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-courtyard-room` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  他挑了朝院子的那间单间，付了一个月押金和半个月房租，房东点清钱才给钥匙。看门人把备用钥匙挂进门房的木板上，嘱咐他晚上十点以后别在楼梯上跺脚。屋里有张床、一只铁炉和半截蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -9 (单间一个月押金与半个月房租（其中4克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1954.02** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Franz Hofer 2提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153; mood -0.05_

  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Otto Berg把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Otto Berg在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1954.09** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home

  赛马场外的泥地上，Berta Stein 2把Josef的工钱数进帽子里，说“下周翻本”。Anna Berg拽了拽Josef的袖子，Josef没有动。回家的电车上，Josef把口袋翻了三遍，只摸到一张赛马票的存根。第二天上工，Josef把工票藏在鞋垫下面，怕家里翻出来。那天夜里，Josef把赛马票的存根烧了，灰留在鞋垫上。
  _effects: cash -12 (第三个栏架前的马（其中12克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09000000000000008; condition; flag +gambling_debt; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005031:child; knowledge “the bookmaker takes bets behind the tram depot”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  _effects: cash -3 (cow milk); condition_

- **1914.02** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban

  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_
